Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The supersymmetry variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 431 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 328 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 431 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the supersymmetry structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 328. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
